MAJOR RISK AND UNCERTAINTIES                                                 主要風險及不確定性

The Board has adopted a board diversity policy which sets out              董事會已採納董事會多元化政策，當中載列實現
the approach to achieve diversity on the Board. The Company                董事會多元化的方法。本公司深知設有多元化董
recognizes the importance and benefits of having a diverse Board           事會的重要性及裨益，並視董事會層面日益多元
and sees increasing diversity at Board level as an essential element       化為實現本公司戰略目標及可持續發展的要素。
in achieving the Company’s strategic goals and sustainable                 本公司務求通過考慮眾多因素以實現董事會多元
development. The Company seeks to achieve Board diversity through          化，該等因素包括但不限於專業知識、技能、經驗、
the consideration of a number of factors, including but not limited to     獨 立 性、年 齡、文 化、種 族 及 性 別。本 公 司 將 考
expertise, skills, experience, independence, age, culture, ethnicity       慮董事會多元化政策及其他因素，同時基於對董
and gender. The Company will select potential Board candidates             事會的潛在貢獻挑選潛在董事會候選人。董事會
based on his or her potential contribution to the Board while taking       的所有委任均以用人唯才為原則，並在充分顧及
into account the board diversity policy and other factors. All Board       董事會多元化的裨益後以客觀標準考慮候選人。
appointments will be based on meritocracy and candidates will

to be appropriate for its business operations. Such systems are            統旨在管理而非消除無法實現業務目標的風險，
designed to manage rather than eliminate the risk of failure to            只能合理而非絕對保證不會出現重大誤報或損失。

not absolute assurance against material misstatement or loss. The          助下，持續監督及審查風險管理及內部控制制度

The Company adopted a dividend policy (the “Dividend Policy”)             本公司採納股息政策（「股息政策」），當中載列有
which sets out the principles and guidelines in relation to the           關宣派、派付及分派股息之原則及指引。在建議
declaration, payment and distribution of dividends. In recommending       或宣派股息時，本公司應考慮（其中包括）董事會
or declaring dividends, the Company shall take into account, among        不時認為屬適當的本集團的經營業績、財務狀況、
other things, the Group’s operating results, financial position, any      對股息的任何法定和監管限制、營運資金需求及
legal and regulatory restrictions on dividends, working capital           其他相關因素。建議派付股息亦須由董事會酌情
requirements and other relevant factors that the Board considers          決定，而宣派任何末期股息均須獲得股東批准。
appropriate from time to time. The proposed payment of dividends          任何未領取的股息應被沒收及應根據細則歸屬本
must also be at the discretion of the Board, and any declaration of       公司。董事會將不時適時檢討股息政策，概不保
final dividend is subject to Shareholders’ approval. Any dividend         證在任何特定期間以任何特定金額宣派或支付股息。
unclaimed shall be forfeited and shall revert to the Company in
accordance with the Articles. The Board will conduct review on
the dividend policy as appropriate from time to time and does not
guarantee any declaration or payment of dividends in any specific
amount for any specific period.

AUDITOR’S RESPONSIBILITIES FOR THE AUDIT OF THE                            核數師就審計綜合財務報表承擔的責
CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL STATEMENTS                                          任
Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance about whether the        我們的目標是對綜合財務報表整體是否不存在由
consolidated financial statements as a whole are free from material        於欺詐或錯誤而導致的重大錯誤陳述取得合理保
misstatement, whether due to fraud or error, and to issue an auditor’s     證，並出具包括我們意見的核數師報告。我們僅
report that includes our opinion. We report our opinion solely to you,     向 閣下（作為整體）報告我們的意見，除此之外
as a body, and for no other purpose. We do not assume responsibility       本報告別無其他目的。我們不會就本報告的內容
towards or accept liability to any other person for the contents of this   向任何其他人士負上或承擔任何責任。合理保證
report. Reasonable assurance is a high level of assurance, but is not      是高水平的保證，但不能保證按照香港審計準則
a guarantee that an audit conducted in accordance with HKSAs will          進行的審核在某一重大錯誤陳述存在時總能被發
always detect a material misstatement when it exists. Misstatements        現。錯誤陳述可以由欺詐或錯誤引起，如果合理
can arise from fraud or error and are considered material if,              預期它們單獨或匯總起來可能影響使用者依賴綜
individually or in the aggregate, they could reasonably be expected to     合財務報表所作出的經濟決定，則有關的錯誤陳
influence the economic decisions of users taken on the basis of these      述可被視作重大。
consolidated financial statements.

As part of an audit in accordance with HKSAs, we exercise                  在根據香港審計準則進行審核的過程中，我們運
professional judgment and maintain professional scepticism                 用了專業判斷，保持了專業懷疑態度。我們亦：
throughout the audit. We also:

•     Identify and assess the risks of material misstatement of the        •   識別和評估由於欺詐或錯誤而導致綜合財
      consolidated financial statements, whether due to fraud or               務報表存在重大錯誤陳述的風險，設計及執
      error, design and perform audit procedures responsive to                 行審核程序以應對該等風險，以及獲取充足
      those risks, and obtain audit evidence that is sufficient and            和適當的審核憑證，作為我們意見的基礎。
      appropriate to provide a basis for our opinion. The risk of              由於欺詐可能涉及串謀、偽造、蓄意遺漏、
      not detecting a material misstatement resulting from fraud is            虛假陳述，或凌駕於內部控制之上，因此未
      higher than for one resulting from error, as fraud may involve           能發現因欺詐而導致的重大錯誤陳述的風
      collusion, forgery, intentional omissions, misrepresentations, or        險高於未能發現因錯誤而導致的重大錯誤
      the override of internal control.                                        陳述的風險。

•     Obtain an understanding of internal control relevant to the          •   了解與審核相關的內部控制，以設計適當的
      audit in order to design audit procedures that are appropriate           審核程序，但目的並非對 貴集團內部控制
      in the circumstances, but not for the purpose of expressing an           的有效性發表意見。
      opinion on the effectiveness of the Group’s internal control.

            changes to how interest received and interest paid are                  及已付利息的呈列方式將有所變動。
            presented. Interest paid will be presented as financing                 已 付 利 息 將 呈 列 為 融 資 現 金 流 量，而
            cash flows and interest received as investing cash flows,               已 收 利 息 將 呈 列 為 投 資 現 金 流 量，此
            which is a change from current presentation as part of                  舉與現時作為經營現金流量一部分的
            operating cash flows.                                                   呈列有所變動。

      The Group’s activities expose it to a variety of financial risks:         本集團業務面臨多種財務風險：市場風險（包
      market risk (including foreign exchange risk, cash flow and               括外匯風險、現金流量及公平值利率風險以
      fair value interest rate risk and price risk), credit risk and            及價格風險）、信貸風險及流動資金風險。
      liquidity risk. The Group’s overall risk management programme             本集團的整體風險管理計劃專注於金融市
      focuses on the unpredictability of financial markets and seeks            場的不可預測性，並尋求盡量降低對本集團
      to minimise potential adverse effects on the Group’s financial            財務表現可能構成的不利影響。
      performance.

      Group entity.

       external rating agency report and forward-looking information             資料。與已知面臨財務困難或對收回應收款

3     FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT (Continued)                                3   財務風險管理（續）
3.2 Capital risk management                                                3.2 資本風險管理
      The Group’s objectives when managing capital are to safeguard            本集團管理資金的目標旨在保障本集團按
      the Group’s ability to continue as a going concern in order              持續經營基準繼續營運的能力，以為股東帶
      to provide returns for shareholders and benefits for other               來回報，同時兼顧其他利益相關方的利益，
      stakeholders and to maintain an optimal capital structure to             並維持最佳的資本架構以減低資本成本。
      reduce the cost of capital.

      In order to maintain or adjust the capital structure, the Group          為維持或調整資本架構，本集團可能調整向
      may adjust the amount of dividends paid to shareholders,                 股東支付的股息金額、向股東退回資本、發
      return capital to shareholders, issue new shares or sell assets          行新股份或出售資產，從而減少債務。
      to reduce debt.

      The Group monitors capital on the basis of the gearing ratio.            本集團根據資本負債比率監控資本。該比率
      This ratio is calculated as net debt divided by total capital. Net       按債務淨額除以總資本計算。債務淨額按借
      debt is calculated as total borrowings and lease liabilities less        款總額及租賃負債減現金及現金等價物以
      cash and cash equivalents and restricted bank deposits. Total            及受限制銀行存款計算。總資本按綜合財務
      capital is calculated as “equity” as shown in the consolidated           狀況表所示的「權益」加債務淨額計算。
      statement of financial position plus net debt.

      The gearing ratios at 31 December 2023 and 2024 were as                  於 2023 年 及 2024 年 12 月 31 日 的 資 本 負 債
      follows:                                                                 比率如下：

                                                                                                於 12 月 31 日

                                                                                            2024 年          2023 年

3.3 Fair value estimation                                                   3.3 公平值估計
(i)   Fair value hierarchy                                                  (i)   公平值層級
      This section explains the judgments and estimates made in                   本節闡述釐定於財務報表中確認及按公平
      determining the fair values of the financial instruments that               值計量的金融工具公平值所作出的判斷及
      are recognised and measured at fair value in the financial                  估計。為得出有關釐定公平值所用輸入數據
      statements. To provide an indication about the reliability of               的可靠性指標，本集團將其金融工具分為三
      the inputs used in determining fair value, the Group has                    個層級：
      categorised its financial instruments into three levels as follows:

      Level 1:    The fair value of financial instruments traded in               第一層： 在活躍市場上買賣的金融工具（如
                  active markets (such as publicly traded derivatives                  公開買賣的衍生工具及股本證券）
                  and equity securities) is based on quoted market                     的公平值乃按於報告期末的市場
                  prices at the end of the reporting period. The                       報價釐定。本集團所持有金融資
                  quoted market price used for financial assets                        產採用的市場報價為當時買盤價。
                  held by the Group is the current bid price. These                    該等工具計入第一層。
                  instruments are included in level 1.

      Level 2:    The fair value of financial instruments that are not            第二層： 未在活躍市場買賣的金融工具（例
                  traded in an active market (for example, over-the-                   如場外衍生工具）的公平值利用
                  counter derivatives) is determined using valuation                   估值技術釐定，該等估值技術盡
                  techniques which maximise the use of observable                      量利用可觀察市場數據，並盡量
                  market data and rely as little as possible on entity-                減少依賴實體特定估計。倘計量
                  specific estimates. If all significant inputs required               工具公平值所需所有重大輸入數
                  to fair value an instrument are observable, the                      據均可觀察，則該工具計入第二
                  instrument is included in level 2.                                   層。

      Level 3:    If one or more of the significant inputs is not based           第三層： 倘一項或多項重大輸入數據並非
                  on observable market data, the instrument is                         根據可觀察市場數據得出，則該
                  included in level 3. This is the case for investment                 工具計入第三層。對於並無可觀
                  in certain WMPs without observable market data.                      察市場數據的若干理財產品投資
                                                                                       而言亦是如此。

(d)   Accounting policies of revenue recognition                         (d)   收入確認的會計政策
      Revenue is recognised when or as the control of goods or                 收入於貨品或服務的控制權轉移至客戶時
      services is transferred to the customer. Depending on the terms          確認。視乎合約條款及適用於該合約的法律
      of the contract and the laws that apply to the contract, revenue         規定，收入可隨時間或於某一時間點確認。
      may be recognised over time or at a point in time.

      Control of the goods or services is transferred over time if the         倘本集團符合下列其中一項情況，貨品或服
      Group:                                                                   務的控制權將隨時間轉移：

      –     provides all of the benefits received and consumed                 －     提供所有獲客戶同時收取並耗用的利
            simultaneously by the customer;                                          益；

      –     creates or enhances an asset that the customer controls            －     因本集團履約而產生或增加客戶所控
            as the Group performs; or                                                制的資產；或

      –     does not create an asset with an alternative use to                －     並無產生對本集團而言有其他用途的
            the Group and the Group has an enforceable right to                      資 產，而 本 集 團 可 強 制 執 行 其 權 利 收
            payment for performance completed to date.                               回至今已完成履約的款項。

(d)   Accounting policies of revenue recognition (Continued)                 (d)   收入確認的會計政策（續）
      If control of the goods and services transfers over time, revenue            倘貨品及服務的控制權隨時間轉移，則收入
      is recognised on a straight-line basis over the terms agreed                 按合約協定的期限或電視節目或播放期間
      on the contracts or over the TV show or broadcasting period.                 以直線法確認。否則，收入於客戶獲得貨品
      Otherwise, revenue is recognised at a point in time when the                 及服務控制權的某一時間點確認。
      customer obtains control of the goods and services.

      The progress towards satisfaction of the performance obligation              履約責任的進度按最能描述本集團履行履
      is measured based on time-based measure of progress                          約責任表現的以時間為基礎的進度計量方
      that best depicts the Group’s performance in satisfying the                  式計量。
      performance obligation.

      Contracts with customers may include multiple performance                    與客戶訂立的合約可能包含多項履約責任。
      obligations. For such arrangements, the Group allocates                      就該等安排而言，本集團按其相對獨立的售
      revenue to each performance obligation based on its relative                 價為每項履約責任分配收入。本集團一般根
      standalone selling price. The Group generally determines                     據向客戶收取的價格釐定獨立售價。倘獨立
      standalone selling prices based on the prices charged to                     售價無法直接觀察得出，則會視乎可觀察資
      customers. If the standalone selling price is not directly                   料的可用性，使用預計成本加利潤或經調整
      observable, it is estimated using expected cost plus a margin                市場評估法進行估算。在估算各項不同履約
      or adjusted market assessment approach, depending on                         責任的相對售價時已作出假設及估計，倘更
      the availability of observable information. Assumptions and                  改對該等假設及估計的判斷，則可能會影響
      estimations have been made in estimating the relative selling                收入確認。
      price of each distinct performance obligation, and changes in
      judgments on these assumptions and estimates may impact
      the revenue recognition.

      If a customer pays consideration or the Group has a right to                 在本集團向客戶轉讓貨品或服務前，倘若客
      an amount of consideration that is unconditional, before the                 戶支付代價或本集團擁有收取代價金額的
      Group transfers the goods or services to the customer, the                   無條件權利，本集團於作出付款或入賬應收
      Group presents the contract liability when the payment is made               款 項 時（以 較 早 者 為 準）呈 列 合 約 負 債。合
      or a receivable is recorded (whichever is earlier). A contract               約負債是本集團因本集團已收取代價（或已
      liability is the Group’s obligation to transfer goods or services to         可向客戶收取到期代價金額）而須向客戶轉
      a customer for which the Group has received consideration (or                讓貨品或服務的責任。

             customer, which is generally upon the acceptance of the                   認。本 集 團 確 認 的 收 入 相 當 於 合 約 銷

36 RELATED PARTY TRANSACTIONS                                        36 關聯方交易
   Parties are considered to be related if one party has the           倘一方有能力直接或間接控制另一方，或在
   ability, directly or indirectly, to control the other party or      作出財務及經營決策方面能對另一方行使
   exercise significant influence over the other party in making       重大影響力，則雙方被視為關聯方。倘雙方
   financial and operating decisions. Parties are also considered      受共同控制、共同重大影響或聯合控制，亦
   to be related if they are subject to common control, common         被視為關聯方。
   significant influence or joint control.

   As mentioned in note 1, the Company is jointly controlled           誠 如 附 註 1 所 述，本 公 司 由 控 股 股 東（即 馬
   by the controlling shareholders, being Ms. Ma, Hsin-Ting,           心婷女士、楊峻榮先生、葉惠美女士及陳中
   Mr. Yang, Chun-Jung, Ms. Yeh Hui-Mei and Mr. Chen,                  先生）共同控制，彼等根據一致行動人士協
   Chung, who are acting in concert pursuant to a concert party        議一致行動。各控股股東、主要管理成員及
   agreement. Each of the controlling shareholders, members of         其近親家庭成員（包括由彼等控制或可行使
   key management and their close family members, including            重大影響力的公司）均被視為本集團的關聯
   companies controlled by them or they can exercise significant       方。
   influence, are considered as related parties of the Group.

   In the opinion of the directors, the related party transactions     董事認為，關聯方交易乃於正常業務過程中
   were carried out in normal course of business and at terms          按本集團與各關聯方磋商之條款進行。
   negotiated between the Group and the respective related
   parties.

40 SUMMARY OF OTHER POTENTIALLY MATERIAL                                    40 其他可能重大會計政策概要
   ACCOUNTING POLICIES
40.1 Subsidiaries                                                           40.1 附屬公司
     Subsidiaries are all entities over which the Group has control.           附屬公司指本集團擁有控制權的所有實體。
     The Group controls an entity where the Group is exposed to,               倘本集團因參與實體營運而承擔風險或有
     or has rights to, variable returns from its involvement with the          權享有當中的可變動回報，且能夠透過其管
     entity and has the ability to affect those returns through its            理該實體業務的權力影響該等回報，則本集
     power to direct the activities of the entity. Subsidiaries are fully      團控制該實體。附屬公司自控制權轉移至本
     consolidated from the date on which control is transferred to             集團之日起全面綜合入賬，自控制權終止日
     the Group. They are deconsolidated from the date that control             期起終止綜合入賬。
     ceases.

     The acquisition method of accounting is used to account for               本集團使用會計收購法為並非共同控制下
     business combinations not under common control by the                     的業務合併入賬。
     Group.

     Inter-company transactions, balances and unrealised gains                 公司間交易、結餘及集團公司間交易的未變
     on transactions between group companies are eliminated.                   現收益均予對銷。除非交易提供證據顯示所
     Unrealised losses are also eliminated unless the transaction              轉讓資產已減值，否則未變現虧損亦會對銷。
     provides evidence of an impairment of the transferred asset.              附屬公司的會計政策已於必要時作出更改，
     Accounting policies of subsidiaries have been changed where               確保與本集團所採納的政策一致。
     necessary to ensure consistency with the policies adopted by
     the Group.

     Non-controlling interests in the results and equity of                    附屬公司業績及權益內的非控股權益於綜
     subsidiaries are shown separately in the consolidated financial           合財務報表中單獨列示。
     statements.

40.2 Separate financial statements                                          40.2 獨立財務報表
     Investments in subsidiaries are accounted for at cost less                於附屬公司的投資按成本扣除減值列賬。成
     impairment. Cost includes direct attributable costs of                    本包括直接應佔投資成本。附屬公司的業績
     investment. The results of subsidiaries are accounted for by the          乃由本公司按已收股息及應收款項基準入賬。
     Company on the basis of dividend received and receivable.

     Impairment testing of the investments in subsidiaries is                  倘股息超出附屬公司宣派股息期間全面收
     required upon receiving a dividend from these investments                 益總額，或倘獨立財務報表中投資賬面值超
     if the dividend exceeds the total comprehensive income of                 出綜合財務報表所示投資對象資產淨值（包
     the subsidiary in the period the dividend is declared or if the           括商譽）的賬面值，則須於自該投資收取股
     carrying amount of the investment in the separate financial               息時，對附屬公司的該等投資進行減值測試。
     statements exceeds the carrying amount in the consolidated
     financial statements of the investee’s net assets including
     goodwill.

40.3 Associates                                                             40.3 聯營公司
      Associates are all entities over which the Group has significant         聯營公司指所有本集團對其有重大影響力
      influence but not control or joint control. This is generally the        而無控制權或聯合控制權的實體，而本集團
      case where the Group holds between 20% and 50% of the                    通常持有 20% 至 50% 投票權。
      voting rights.

      Investments in associates                                                於聯營公司的投資
      Under the equity method, the investments is initially recognised         根據權益法，投資初步按成本確認，並會調
      at cost, and the carrying amount is increased or decreased to            高或調低賬面值以確認於收購日期後投資
      recognise the investor’s share of profit or loss of the investee         者應佔投資對象的損益及應佔投資對象的
      and the share of other comprehensive income of the investee              其他全面收益。已收或應收聯營公司股息確
      after the date of acquisition. Dividends received or receivable          認為投資賬面值的扣減項。本集團於該等聯
      from associates are recognised as a reduction in the carrying            營公司的投資包括收購時確認的商譽，經扣
      amount of the investments. The Group’s investments in these              除任何累計減值虧損。收購一家聯營公司所
      associates include goodwill identified on acquisition, net of            有權權益後，該聯營公司的成本與本集團應
      any accumulated impairment loss. Upon the acquisition of the             佔聯營公司可識別資產及負債淨公平值之
      ownership interest in an associate, any difference between the           間的任何差額作為商譽入賬。
      cost of the associate and the Group’s share of the net fair value
      of the associate’s identifiable assets and liabilities is accounted
      for as goodwill.

      If the ownership interest in an associate is reduced but                 如果聯營公司的所有者權益減少但仍對其
      significant influence is retained, only a proportionate share of         存在重大影響，只有按比例將之前在其他綜
      the amounts previously recognised in other comprehensive                 合損益中確認的數額重新分類至綜合全面
      income or loss is reclassified to consolidated statements of             損益表（如適當）。
      comprehensive income or loss where appropriate.

40.4 Segment reporting                                                       40.4 分部報告
      Operating segments are reported in a manner consistent                        經營分部的報告方式須與主要經營決策者
      with the internal reporting provided to the chief operating                  （「主要經營決策者」）獲提供的內部報告方式
      decision-makers (“CODMs”). The CODMs, who are responsible                     一致。負責分配資源及評估經營分部表現的
      for allocating resources and assessing performance of the                     主要經營決策者被識別為作出策略性決策
      operating segments, have been identified as the Executive                     的執行董事。
      Directors that make strategic decisions.

40.5 Foreign currency translation                                            40.5 外幣換算
(a)   Functional and presentation currency                                   (a)   功能及呈列貨幣
      Items included in the financial statements of each of the                    本集團旗下的各公司的財務資料所包括的
      companies comprising the Group are measured using the                        項目乃使用該等公司經營所在的主要經濟
      currency of the primary economic environment in which these                  環境的貨幣（「功能貨幣」）計量。本公司及中
      companies operate (the “functional currency”). The functional                國 境 外 公 司 的 功 能 貨 幣 為 港 元（「港 元」）及
      currency of the Company and the companies outside of PRC                     台幣「台幣」）。中國境內公司的功能貨幣為
      is Hong Kong dollar (“HK$”) and Taiwan dollar (“TW$”). The                   人民幣。綜合財務報表以本集團的呈列貨幣
      functional currency of the companies in PRC is RMB. The                      人民幣呈列。
      consolidated financial statements are presented in RMB which
      is the Group’s presentation currency.

      exchange rates of monetary assets and liabilities denominated                額」內確認。
      in foreign currencies are recognised in the consolidated
      statement of comprehensive income within “other gains, net”.

      Non-monetary items that are measured at fair value in a foreign              以公平值計量的外幣非貨幣項目採用釐定

      when the fair value was determined. Translation differences on               產及負債的匯兌差額列報為公平值損益的

     attributable to the acquisition of the items.

     Subsequent costs are included in the asset’s carrying amount         僅當有關項目的未來經濟利益可能流入本

     to the consolidated statement of comprehensive income during

       improvements              useful lives and the lease terms                          期之較短者

     The assets’ residual values and useful lives are reviewed, and       資產的剩餘價值及可使用年期在各財務狀

     than its estimated recoverable amount (Note 40.9).                   註 40.9）。

40.9 Impairment of non-financial assets                                    40.9 非金融資產減值
      Assets that are subject to amortisation are tested for                     當發生事件或情況轉變，顯示賬面值可能無
      impairment whenever events or changes in circumstances                     法收回時，則對須攤銷的資產進行減值測試。
      indicate that the carrying amount may not be recoverable. An               減值虧損按資產賬面值超出其可收回金額
      impairment loss is recognised for the amount by which the                  的金額確認。可收回金額為資產公平值減銷
      asset’s carrying amount exceeds its recoverable amount. The                售成本與使用價值兩者的較高者。於評估減
      recoverable amount is the higher of an asset’s fair value less             值時，資產按獨立可識別現金流入的最低層
      costs to sell and value in use. For the purposes of assessing              次分組，有關現金流入大致上獨立於其他資
      impairment, assets are grouped at the lowest levels for which              產或資產組別（現金產生單位）的現金流入。
      there are separately identifiable cash inflows which are largely           在各報告期末，將審閱出現減值的非金融資
      independent of the cash inflows from other assets or groups of             產（商譽除外）是否有撥回減值的可能。
      assets (cash-generating units). Non-financial assets other than
      goodwill that suffered an impairment are reviewed for possible
      reversal of the impairment at the end of each reporting period.

(d)   Impairment (Continued)                                                (d)   減值（續）
      Impairment of other receivables are measured as either                      其他應收款項減值乃按 12 個月預期信貸虧
      12-month expected credit losses or lifetime expected credit                 損或全期預期信貸虧損計量，取決於信貸風
      losses, depending on whether there has been a significant                   險自初始確認起是否顯著增加。倘應收款項
      increase in credit risk since initial recognition. If a significant         的信貸風險自初始確認起顯著增加，減值按
      increase in credit risk of a receivable has occurred since initial          全期預期信貸虧損計量。
      recognition, impairment is measured as lifetime expected credit

40.11 Offsetting financial instruments                                      40.11 抵銷金融工具
      Financial assets and liabilities are offset and the net amount              當有法定可強制執行權利可抵銷已確認金
      reported in the consolidated statement of financial position                額，並有意按淨額基準結算或同時變現資產
      when there is a legally enforceable right to offset the recognised          及償付負債時，金融資產與負債可互相抵銷，
      amounts and there is an intention to settle on a net basis or               並於綜合財務狀況表呈報其淨額。法定可強
      realise the asset and settle the liability simultaneously. The              制執行權利不得依賴未來事件而定，而在一
      legally enforceable right must not be contingent on future                  般業務過程中以及倘本集團或對手方一旦
      events and must be enforceable in the normal course of                      出現違約、無償債能力或破產時亦必須可強
      business and in the event of default, insolvency or bankruptcy              制執行。
      of the Group or the counterparty.

40.12 Inventories                                                           40.12 存貨
      Inventories are stated at the lower of cost and net realisable              存貨按成本與可變現淨值兩者中的較低者
      value. Cost is determined using the weighted average method.                列賬。成本使用加權平均法釐定。採購存貨
      Costs of purchased inventory are determined after deducting                 的成本於扣除回扣及折扣後釐定。可變現淨
      rebates and discounts. Net realisable value is the estimated                值乃於日常業務過程中的估計售價減去適
      selling price in the ordinary course of business, less the                  用可變銷售開支。
      applicable variable selling expense.

40.16 Trade and other payables                                               40.16 貿易及其他應付款項
     Trade payables are obligations to pay for goods or services that           貿易應付款項為於日常業務過程中向供應
     have been acquired in the ordinary course of business from                 商購入貨品或服務的付款責任。倘貿易及其
     suppliers. Trade and other payables are classified as current              他應付款項於一年或以內（或如屬較長時間，
     liabilities if payment is due within one year or less (or in the           則以業務正常經營週期為準）到期，則分類
     normal operating cycle of the business if longer). If not, they            為流動負債，否則按非流動負債呈列。
     are presented as non-current liabilities.

     Trade and other payables are recognised initially at fair value            貿易及其他應付款項初步按公平值確認，其
     and subsequently measured at amortised cost using the                      後使用實際利率法按攤銷成本計量。

40.17 Borrowings and borrowing costs                                         40.17 借款及借款成本
     Borrowings are initially recognised at fair value, net of                  借款初步按公平值確認，經扣除已產生的交
     transaction costs incurred. Borrowings are subsequently                    易成本。借款其後以攤銷成本列賬。所得款
     measured at amortised cost. Any difference between the                     項（經 扣 除 交 易 成 本）與 贖 回 金 額 之 間 的 任
     proceeds (net of transaction costs) and the redemption amount              何差額，使用實際利率法於借款期內在損益
     is recognised in profit or loss over the period of the borrowings          中確認。於建立貸款融資時支付的費用於將
     using the effective interest method. Fees paid on the                      很有可能提取部分或全部融資時確認為貸
     establishment of loan facilities are recognised as transaction             款的交易成本。於此情況下，有關費用遞延
     costs of the loan to the extent that it is probable that some or           至融資被提取時。倘無證據顯示將很有可能
     all of the facility will be drawn down. In this case, the fee is           提取部分或全部融資，有關費用將被資本化
     deferred until the draw-down occurs. To the extent there is no             為流動資金服務的預付款項並於相關融資
     evidence that it is probable that some or all of the facility will be      期間進行攤銷。
     drawn down, the fee is capitalised as a prepayment for liquidity
     services and amortised over the period of the facility to which it
     relates.

     Borrowings are removed from the balance sheet when the                     當合約中規定的責任獲解除、取消或屆滿時，
     obligation specified in the contract is discharged, cancelled              借款自資產負債表中剔除。金融負債賬面值
     or expired. The difference between the carrying amount of a               （已消除或轉撥至另一方）與已付代價（包括
     financial liability that has been extinguished or transferred to           已轉讓的任何非現金資產或所承擔負債）之
     another party and the consideration paid, including any non-               間的差額於損益中確認為融資成本。
     cash assets transferred or liabilities assumed, is recognised in
     profit or loss as finance costs.

     Borrowings are classified as current liabilities unless the Group          除非本集團有無條件權利將負債的結算遞
     has an unconditional right to defer settlement of the liability for        延 至 報 告 期 後 至 少 12 個 月，否 則 借 款 分 類
     at least 12 months after the reporting period.                             為流動負債。

      in the consolidated statements of comprehensive income,                    收益表中確認，惟與於其他全面收益或直接
      except to the extent that it relates to items recognised in other          於權益中確認的項目相關者除外。於此情況
      comprehensive income or directly in equity. In this case,                  下，稅項亦分別於其他全面收益或直接於權

      statement of financial position date in the country where the              期已頒佈或實質已頒佈的稅法計算。管理層
      Group operates and generates taxable income. Management                    就適用稅務法例有待詮釋的情況定期評估
      periodically evaluates positions taken in tax returns with respect         報稅表的狀況，並於適用情況下根據預期須
      to situations in which applicable tax regulation is subject to             向稅務機構支付的稅款設定撥備。
      interpretation. It establishes provisions where appropriate on

      differences and it is probable that the differences will not

40.20 Provisions                                                           40.20 撥備
      Provisions are recognised when: the Group has a present                    倘本集團因過往事件而承擔現有法定或推
      legal or constructive obligation as a result of past events; it is         定責任；有可能須流出資源以履行責任；且
      probable that an outflow of resources will be required to settle           有關金額已可靠估計時，則會確認撥備。概
      the obligation; and the amount has been reliably estimated.                不會就未來經營虧損確認撥備。
      Provisions are not recognised for future operating losses.

      Where there are a number of similar obligations, the likelihood            倘出現多項類似責任，履行責任時須流出資
      that an outflow will be required in settlement is determined by            源的可能性乃經考慮整體責任類別後釐定。
      considering the class of obligations as a whole. A provision is            即使就同一責任類別所包含的任何一個項
      recognised even if the likelihood of an outflow with respect to            目流出資源的可能性極低，仍須確認撥備。
      any one item included in the same class of obligations may be
      small.

      Provisions are measured at the present value of management’s               撥備按管理層對預期履行有關責任所需開
      best estimate of the expenditures expected to be required to               支的最佳估計現值採用稅前利率計量，該利
      settle the obligation using a pre-tax rate that reflects current           率反映當時市場對金錢時間值和有關責任
      market assessments of the time value of money and the risks                特定風險的評估。因時間推移而增加的撥備
      specific to the obligation. The increase in the provision due to           確認為財務成本開支。
      passage of time is recognised as finance cost expense.

40.21 Earnings per share                                                   40.21 每股盈利
(a)   Basic earnings per share                                             (a)   每股基本盈利
      Basic earnings per share is calculated by dividing:                        每股基本盈利乃按以下方式計算得出：

      •     the profit attributable to owners of the company,                    •    公司擁有人應佔溢利（不包括普通股以
            excluding any costs of servicing equity other than                        外的任何權益服務成本），除以
            ordinary shares; and

      •     by the weighted average number of ordinary shares                    •    財政年度內發行在外普通股加權平均
            outstanding during the financial year, adjusted for bonus                 數，並就年內已發行普通股（不包括庫
            elements in ordinary shares issued during the year and                    存股）的股利成分調整。
            excluding treasury shares.

      The Group, as a lessee, leases offices premises with lease                本集團作為承租人租賃辦公場所，租賃期為
      terms from 2 to 10 years. Leases are recognised as a right-               2 至 10 年。租賃在租賃資產可供本集團使用
      of-use asset and corresponding liability at the date of which             之日確認為使用權資產及相應負債。租賃產
      the leased asset is available for use by the Group. Assets                生的資產及負債初步按現值計量。
      and liabilities arising from a lease are initially measured on a

      Lease liabilities include the net present value of the following          租賃負債包括以下租賃付款的現值淨額（如
      lease payments, where applicable:                                         適用）：

      (a)   fixed payments (including in-substance fixed payments),             (a)   固定付款（包括實質性固定付款）減任
            less any lease incentives receivable;                                     何應收租賃優惠；

      (b)   variable lease payment that are based on an index or a              (b)   以指數或利率為基準的可變租賃付款；
            rate;

      (c)   amounts expected to be payable by the lessee under                  (c)   剩餘價值擔保下的承租人預期應付款
            residual value guarantees;                                                項；

      (d)   the exercise price of a purchase option if the lessee is            (d)   購買權的行使價格（倘承租人合理地確
            reasonably certain to exercise that option; and                           定行使該權利）；及

            lease term reflects the lessee exercising that option.                    承租人行使該選擇權）。

      is allocated between the liability and finance cost. The finance       財務成本。財務成本於租期內自損益扣除，

      balance of the liability for each period.

            commencement date less any lease incentives received,                款，減去所得任何租賃優惠，

      •     any initial direct costs, and                                    •   任何初始直接成本，及

      •     restoration costs.                                               •   修復成本。

      Right-of-use assets are generally depreciated over the shorter         使用權資產通常於資產的可使用年期及租
      of the asset’s useful life and the lease term on a straight-line       賃 期（以 較 短 者 為 準）按 直 線 法 折 舊。倘 本
      basis. If the Group is reasonably certain to exercise a purchase       集團合理確定行使購買權，則使用權資產於
      option, the right-of-use asset is depreciated over the underlying      相關資產的可使用年期進行折舊。
      asset’s useful life.

Governance Structure                                                    管治架構
The Board has overall responsibility for the Group’s ESG strategy and   董事會全面負責本集團的 ESG 策略及匯報工作。
reporting. The Board is responsible for evaluating and determining      董事會負責評估及釐定本集團的 ESG 相關風險，

in place. The executive directors of the Company are responsible        戰略、目標及年度報告，並推動相關實施。彼等

identify, evaluate, review, and manage major ESG issues, risks and      團 ESG 管理政策及策略項下的多項 ESG 相關任務。

A.   ENVIRONMENT                                                         A.   環境
     As our business does not involve manufacturing and production            由於我們的業務不涉及製造及生產活動，且
     activities, and a large portion of resources are provided by our         大部分資源由我們的供應商及第三方物流
     suppliers and third-party logistics service providers, therefore         服務供應商提供，因此我們的營運對環境並
     our operations do not have material negative impacts on                  無重大負面影響。儘管如此，本集團重視在
     the environment. Nevertheless, the Group attaches great                  業務穩健營運的同時盡量減少其環境足跡，
     importance to minimising its environmental footprint while               推進經濟、社會及環境平衡及可持續發展。
     maintaining stable business operations, to promote balanced              本集團履行環境責任並嚴格遵循環境法律
     and sustainable development of economy, society and                      及法規，亦致力將環境保護的理念納入其營
     environment. The Group fulfils its environmental responsibilities        運及決策過程中。
     and strictly abides by environmental laws and regulations. It is
     also committed to incorporating the concept of environmental
     protection into its operations and decision making processes.

     To effectively control its negative impact on the environment,           為有效管控本集團對環境造成的負面影響，
     the Group has continuously strengthened and improved its                 本集團透過促進資源的有效使用、提高自然
     environmental management by facilitating efficient use of                資源的有效利用、減少廢棄物及污染以及應
     resources, enhancing efficient utilisation of natural resources,         對氣候變化，持續加強及完善其環境管理，
     reducing waste and pollution, and combating climate change,              以推動生態及環境保護。
     so as to promote ecological and environmental conservation.

     The Group is in the process of arranging and assigning                   本集團正安排及指派負責部門及人員，進一
     responsible departments and personnel to further improve our             步完善我們的數據收集系統。我們並無制定
     data collection system. We did not establish any quantitative            任何量化目標，但制定初步的方向性目標及
     targets but set preliminary directional targets and emission             減排計劃，以減少本集團的排放量及對環境
     reduction plans to reduce the Group’s emissions and                      的影響。展望未來，我們將考慮行業表現、
     environmental impact. Looking ahead, we will formulate the               過去、現在及未來環境表現等多方面因素，
     following quantitative reduction targets by considering various          制定以下量化減排目標。
     factors such as industry performance and past, current and
     future environmental performances.

1.    Emissions                                                            1.    排放物
1.1   Air Pollutants Emissions                                             1.1   空氣污染物排放
      The Group is principally engaged in new retail business and                本 集 團 主 要 從 事 新 零 售 業 務 及 IP 創 造 及 營
      IP creation and operation business. We engage third-party                  運業務。我們在營運中委聘第三方生產商及
      manufacturers and logistics service providers in our operation.            物流服務供應商。由於本集團的業務性質，
      Due to the Group’s business nature, no material air pollutants             在日常業務過程中並無產生重大空氣污染物，
      are produced in the ordinary course of business, and the air               而本集團產生的空氣污染物排放主要來自
      pollutant emissions generated by the Group are mainly sourced              辦公室營運中流動來源的柴油及汽油燃燒，
      from diesel and gasoline combustion from mobile sources in                 以及員工宿舍爐灶的天然氣燃燒。由於我們
      office operation, and natural gas combustion from stove in                 主要依賴第三方物流服務供應商運輸產品，
      staff quarters. As we mainly rely on third-party logistics service         我們並無擁有任何貨車。本集團已實施以下
      providers for transporting products, we do not own any cargo               節能措施以減少空氣污染物排放：
      trucks. The Group has implemented the following energy saving
      measures to reduce air pollutants emissions:

      •     Plan the driving routes in advance before departure;                 •   出發前提前規劃行車路線；

      •     Stop and turn off the engine when parking; and                       •   停車時停好車並關閉發動機；及

      •     Conduct regular repair and maintenance for better energy             •   進 行 定 期 維 修 及 保 養，以 提 高 能 源 使
            use efficiency.                                                          用效率。

         1.      The calculation of the air pollutant emission is referenced from         1.     空氣污染物排放的計算乃參考以下指引及

2.       Use of Resources                                                          2.    資源使用
2.1     Energy Consumption                                                         2.1   能源消耗
        The Group recognises its responsibility to take the initiative in                本集團認識到其有責任主動有效利用有限
        efficiently utilising finite resources and carries out its corporate             資源，並履行其企業社會責任，引入額外的
        social responsibility to introduce additional eco-friendly                       環保方法，以提高本集團的可持續發展表現。
        approaches to enhance the Group’s sustainability performance.                    本集團致力提高能源效益及提倡節約資源。
        The Group is committed to improving energy efficiency and                        透過在各業務分部實施適當的能源及材料
        advocating resource conservation. By implementing appropriate                    管理計劃，我們可積極影響環境及經濟，同
        energy and material management programmes in each                                時確保業務可持續發展。
        business segment, we can positively influence our environment
        and economy while also ensuring business sustainability.

         Due to the nature of our business as mentioned in “1.                           由於「1. 排放物」所述的業務性質，於日常營

4.   Climate Change                                                        4.   氣候變化
     In recent years, climate change has brought more frequent and              近年，氣候變化為地球帶來更多、破壞力更
     destructive extreme weather crises to the globe, which results             強的極端天氣等危機，為人類及商業社會造
     in serious losses for human beings and the commercialised                  成了嚴重的損失。本集團意識到氣候變化對
     society. The Group recognises climate change poses potential               其業務營運構成潛在風險，包括極端天氣加
     risks to its business operations, including policy, reputation and         劇、海平面上升、持續熱浪以及低碳轉型所
     market changes caused by intensifying extreme weather, rising              導致的政策、聲譽及市場變化，可能影響其
     sea level, chronic heat waves and low-carbon transition, which             業務表現，同時增加可持續發展的不穩定性。
     may affect its business performance and add instability to its
     sustainable development.

     Based on the business nature and geographical location, the                本集團針對業務性質及地理位置，已初步識
     Group has initially identified relevant climate risks, and plans to        別相關氣候風險，並計劃於未來進行更深入
     conduct a more in-depth climate risk assessment in the future              的氣候風險評估，進一步分析有關風險的影
     to further analyse the impact of relevant risks. To reduce the             響。為降低氣候變化對業務的影響，本集團
     impact of climate change on business, the Group will formulate             會於日後因應不同的風險制訂進一步應對

5.       Employment and Labour Practices                                    5.    僱傭及勞工常規
5.1     Employment                                                          5.1   僱傭
         Recruitment and equal opportunity                                        招聘及平等機會
         Our success depends on our ability to attract, retain and                我們的成功取決於我們吸引、挽留及激勵合
         motivate qualified personnel. We have invested significant               資格人員的能力。我們已投入大量資源為本
         resources in the recruiting of the suitable candidates for our           公司招募合適候選人。我們主要透過網上渠
         Company. We primarily recruit our employees through online               道及社交網絡平台招聘僱員，並根據《中華
         channels and social networking platforms and enter into                  人民共和國勞動合同法》與長期僱員訂立規
         standardized employment contracts with our permanent                     範的僱傭合約。
         employees in accordance with the Labor Contract Law of the
         PRC* (《中華人民共和國勞動合同法》).

         In addition, we are committed to offering a fair and caring              此外，我們致力於為僱員提供公平及關愛員
         working environment to our employees. We have established                工的工作環境。我們已制定涵蓋人力資源管
         internal control policies covering various aspects of human              理各個方面（包括招聘、培訓、職業道德及
         resource management including recruitment, trainings,                    法律合規）的內部控制政策。我們採用高招
         work ethics and legal compliance. We adopt high standards                聘標準，訂有嚴格的程序，以確保新聘員工
         in recruitment with strict procedures to ensure the quality              的質素。我們擁有平等的機會及反對歧視。

                                       •   接獲舉報後立即回應並調查舉報案件；及

                                           受到傷害。

        development.

        The Group has all along been concerned about poverty relief,              本集團一直關注與社區有關的扶貧、健康生
        healthy living and environmental sustainability in relation               活及環境可持續性，並支持聚焦上述領域的
        to the community, and supports initiatives which focus on                 活動。於報告期間，本集團向公共衛生、社
        the forementioned areas. The Group has made charitable                    會福利及青少年發展領域的多個機構及項

        relation to public health, social welfare and youth development,

        Case Sharing – Being the Cooperative Unit of the Low-Carb Medical Conference*
        ( 全國低碳醫學大會 ) for four consecutive years
        案例分享－連續四年成為全國低碳醫學大會合作單位


        The 6th Low-Carb Medical Conference was successfully held on 15 December 2024. All along, the Group has been
        committed to promoting the new health concept of “MODONG New Life-style* ( 魔 胴 新 生 活 )”, which is a life-style of
        balanced nutrition, low-carbon diet, scientific body-building, staying hydrated, getting enough sleep and timely releasing
        stress.
        第六屆低碳醫學大會於 2024 年 12 月 15 日成功舉辦。本集團一直以來致力於推廣「魔胴新生活」新健康概念。「魔
        胴新生活」是一種均衡營養、低碳飲食、科學健身、補充水分、充足睡眠、及時釋放壓力的生活方式。

        We have been serving as the cooperative unit of the conference for four consecutive years, playing an important role of a
        promoter of low-carbon diets in promoting the development of low-carbon medical technology and industry.
        我們連續四年成為大會合作單位，為推動低碳醫學技術與產業發展發揮了低碳飲食推動者的重要作用。

248        STAR PLUS LEGEND HOLDINGS LIMITED 巨星傳奇集團有限公司
                                                          Environmental, Social and Governance Report
                                                                              環境、社會及管治報告




       Case Sharing – Caring for the Community Action 2024 – Donating zongzi gift boxes to social welfare organization
       案例分享 – 2024 年社區關愛行動－向社會福利機構捐贈粽子禮盒


       The Group has been actively involved in community charitable initiatives. In July 2024, the Group donated 500 “Ai Chi
       Xian Mo Ren Healthy Zongzi Gift Boxes* ( 愛 吃 鮮 摩 人 健 康 粽 子 禮 盒 )” to the Kunshan Amity Social Organization
       Incubation Center* ( 昆山市愛德社會組織培育中心 ) under the Amity Foundation* ( 愛德基金會 ), aiming to promote
       care and initiatives for disadvantaged groups.
       本集團一直積極參與社區慈善活動。2024 年 7 月，本集團向愛德基金會下轄昆山市愛德社會組織培育中心捐贈
       500 份「愛吃鮮摩人健康粽子禮盒」，旨在促進對弱勢群體的關愛及措施。

       Additionally, in June 2024, the Group donated 45 zongzi gift boxes to Kunshan City’s ‘Blue Sky Rescue Team* ( 藍天救
       援隊 )’ to demonstrate its support for social welfare rescue forces.
       此外，2024 年 6 月，本集團向昆山市「藍天救援隊」捐贈 45 個粽子禮盒，以示對社會公益救援力量的支持。
